- **j5ik2o-takt-optimizer**: 既存のTAKTワークフロー（ワークフローYAML・ファセット群）を最適化するスキル。 トークン消費削減、ステップ統合、ルール簡素化、ファセット再利用促進、 ループ制御の改善、並列化の提案を実施し、最適化後のファイルを直接生成する。 takt-analyzeの診断結果（静的分析・ログ診断）を入力として活用できる。 本スキルは「最適化の実行」のみを担い、診断・分析はtakt-analyzeに委譲する。 references/taktのエンジン仕様・スタイルガイドを基準とする。 トリガー：「ワークフローを最適化」「taktの高速化」「ワークフローを軽くしたい」 「トークンを減らしたい」「ステップを減らしたい」「takt optimize」 「ワークフローの効率化」「ファセットを整理したい」「ワークフローをスリムにして」 「taktのコスト削減」「ワークフローをシンプルにしたい」 ([j5ik2o/ai-tools](https://github.com/j5ik2o/ai-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/takt/skills/j5ik2o-takt-optimizer) · ★28)
- **j5ik2o-takt-skill-updater**: nrslib/takt の新バージョンリリース時に、takt-*スキル群（j5ik2o-takt-task-builder, j5ik2o-takt-workflow-builder, j5ik2o-takt-facet-builder, j5ik2o-takt-analyzer, j5ik2o-takt-optimizer）を最新のtaktバージョンに追従させるスキル。 TypeScriptスキーマ（taskRecordSchemas.ts / taskExecutionSchemas.ts）、ワークフローYAML、ファセットMarkdownの差分を検出し、 SKILL.md・参照ドキュメント（task-schema.md等）を体系的に更新する。 トリガー：「taktスキルを更新」「takt-*スキルの鮮度チェック」「taktバージョンアップ対応」 「スキルが古くないか確認」「takt skill updater」 ([j5ik2o/ai-tools](https://github.com/j5ik2o/ai-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/takt/skills/j5ik2o-takt-skill-updater) · ★28)

- **j6-board-monitor**: J6 开发板资源监控与推理期间资源采集。当用户需要监控 BPU 占用率、DDR 带宽、内存使用时触发。支持三种场景：(1) Scenario A：CV 模型在指定帧率（如 10Hz）推理期间同步采集 BPU/DDR/内存数据；(2) Scenario B：独立监控板端硬件资源（无推理负载）；(3) Scenario C：LLM 模型循环推理期间同步采集资源数据，使用 simple_demo_request 保持模型持续运行。关键词：BPU 监控、DDR 带宽、内存使用、资源监控、设定帧率推理、LLM 推理监控、hrt_ucp_monitor、hrut_ddr、simple_demo_request、板端资源评估。注意：不要用 hbm_infer/gRPC 做高频推理监控（通信开销太大）。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/ucp/j6-board-monitor) · ★31)
- **j6-hbdk-compile**: 通用模型编译 skill。基于 YAML 配置文件驱动，支持 ONNX 和 BC 模型格式。支持从 PTQ config 自动提取输入预处理参数。工作流：生成配置 -&gt; 默认必须经用户确认 -&gt; 再执行编译与报告。Use when the user mentions 模型编译、compile、导出 hbm、生成上板产物、pyramid 输入、resizer 输入。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/hbdk/j6-hbdk-compile) · ★31)
- **j6-hbdk-export-compile**: 为量化后的模型生成导出编译流程代码（export QAT BC → convert → remove_io_op → statistics 检查 CPU 算子 → compile HBM）。务必在用户提到导出编译、HBM 编译、模型部署编译、QAT BC 导出、remove_io_op、CPU 算子检查、模型编译部署、HBM 生成时触发此 skill，即使用户只提到其中一个步骤，只要涉及从 QAT BC 到 HBM 的任何环节都应触发。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-hbdk-generating/j6-hbdk-export-compile) · ★31)
- **j6-hmct-cosine-similarity-tuning**: HMCT PTQ 精度调优工作流。自动按 INT8→INT16→dual-int16→FP16 逐级验证上限， 结合节点敏感度分析渐进回退，找到满足 Cosine Similarity 阈值（默认 &gt;=0.99）的 最小混精度 quant_config，产出达标配置与调优报告。 触发关键词：HMCT 精度调优、PTQ 调优、量化精度、cosine similarity 不达标、混合精度。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/hmct/j6-hmct-cosine-similarity-tuning) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-adaptation**: 为用户的浮点 PyTorch 模型适配地平线机器人公司的 QAT 工具 `horizon_plugin_pytorch`。这是一个编排型 skill，必须按固定顺序依次调用：`j6-plugin-set-march` → `j6-plugin-insert-quant-dequant` → `j6-plugin-dynamic-block` → `j6-plugin-prepare` → `j6-plugin-set-fake-quantize`。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-adaptation) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-consistency-debug**: 当用户遇到 Horizon Plugin PyTorch 训练部署一致性问题（QAT 模型正常但 BC/HBM 掉点、export/convert/compile 阶段精度偏差）时使用。本 skill 引导用户按 qat.pt -&gt; qat.export.pt -&gt; qat.bc -&gt; quantized.bc -&gt; hbm 分段定位问题，并在每个阶段帮助适配工具、分析工具产出物。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-consistency-debug) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-dynamic-block**: 在适配 horizon_plugin_pytorch（prepare/JIT_STRIP）时，自动为动态控制流/动态循环中涉及算子替换或融合的逻辑添加 dynamic_block 标注，避免 scope/替换错乱与 forward 报错。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-adaptation/j6-plugin-dynamic-block) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-export**: 使用 horizon_plugin_pytorch 的 hbdk4.export 将 QAT 模型导出为 HBIR IR 模块。以独立导出脚本的形式执行，不在训练或评测脚本中添加导出逻辑。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-export) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-graph-diff**: 对比两份 FX Graph 计算图文件，精确定位计算图结构和算子参数差异，并根据 diff 报告在原始模型代码中找到对应的差异位置。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-graph-diff) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-hbdk-generating**: 为基础网络结构生成从量化到编译的完整流程代码（set_march → Quant/DeQuant → 量化配置 → prepare → 校准 → QAT → export → convert → remove_io_op → statistics → compile HBM）。当用户需要同时覆盖量化和编译多个步骤时触发，如"帮我写量化编译全流程代码"、"Conv+BN+ReLU 量化部署"、"地平线量化编译"、"基础结构量化到 HBM"。即使用户没有明确说"全流程"，只要涉及从量化到编译的多个步骤都应触发。如果用户只需要量化或只需要编译，应路由到对应子 skill。关键词："量化编译"、"量化部署"、"全流程"、"set_march 到 HBM"、"地平线量化"、"Horizon 量化编译"、"基础结构量化"、"QAT 量化编译"。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-hbdk-generating) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-insert-quant-dequant**: 为浮点 PyTorch 模型插入部署边界 QuantStub/DeQuantStub（horizon_plugin_pytorch）。满足：quant/dequant 是部署边界；每个输入/输出各自独立 stub；QuantStub 初始化不设置 scale。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-adaptation/j6-plugin-insert-quant-dequant) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-model-check-result**: 在 horizon_plugin_pytorch 量化配置检查中，读取并分析 prepare 产出的 model_check_result.txt，定位模型结构与 qconfig 问题（算子融合、共享模块、qconfig 异常、averaging_constant 非默认值、算子输入浮点检查）。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-model-check-result) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-precision-tuning**: 当用户遇到 Horizon Plugin PyTorch 精度调优问题时使用。本 skill 聚焦 PyTorch 侧精度调优，不处理 export、convert、compile 或板端一致性问题。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-precision-tuning) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-prepare**: 在适配 horizon_plugin_pytorch 的过程中对浮点模型执行 prepare（仅添加 prepare 调用；qconfig_setter 固定为全部双 int8 模板；不包含 dynamic_block 相关修改）。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-adaptation/j6-plugin-prepare) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-quantization**: 为基础网络结构生成量化流程代码（set_march → 插入 Quant/DeQuant → 配置量化参数 → prepare → 校准 → QAT 训练）。务必在用户提到模型量化、量化流程、QAT 校准、Horizon 量化适配、HistogramObserver/MinMaxObserver 配置、量化参数配置、校准训练、QuantStub 插入时触发此 skill，即使用户只问其中一个步骤，只要涉及 Horizon 量化流程的任何环节都应触发。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-hbdk-generating/j6-plugin-quantization) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-set-fake-quantize**: 在适配 horizon_plugin_pytorch 的量化流程中，为模型设置 fake quantize 状态（QAT/CALIBRATION/VALIDATION）。只添加/调用 set_fake_quantize，不做其他修改。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-adaptation/j6-plugin-set-fake-quantize) · ★31)
- **j6-plugin-set-march**: 在适配 horizon_plugin_pytorch 的过程中，为脚本或入口添加 march 设置逻辑。 ([HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills](https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/OE-Skills/tree/HEAD/horizon/skills/plugin/j6-plugin-adaptation/j6-plugin-set-march) · ★31)

- **ja-academic-proofreader**: 日本語の学術文（論文・予稿・卒論・修論・ジャーナル投稿・申請書・研究計画書・レポート）を、 田中研の「論文Tips」「日本語Tips」をルールブックとして添削・校正・チェックする。 textlint等で機械検出できる表現（近年・という・〜を行う・連用形・受動態・約物の全半角など）は 同梱スクリプトで検出し、文脈依存のもの（トップヘビー・トピックセンテンス・新情報後回し・主語一致・ 粒度・序論構成・図表の書き方など）はこのスキルのルーブリックに従って判定する。 ユーザーが日本語の論文・予稿・レポート等の「添削」「校正」「推敲」「赤入れ」「Tipsに沿っているか確認」 「日本語をチェック」を求めたら、明示的に "textlint" や "Tips" と言わなくても必ずこのスキルを使う。 .tex / .md / .txt のいずれにも対応。 ([iatosh/dotfiles](https://github.com/iatosh/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/.agents/skills/ja-academic-proofreader) · ★1)

- **ja-review-text**: AI が書いた日本語の文章を、人間が書いたように自然な日本語へ全面的に書き直す。AI 臭さ（テンプレ感、記号過多、過剰な丁寧さ、抽象語の空回り）を完全に除去する。使用トリガー: 'この文章を自然にして', '人間っぽく書き直して', 'AI 臭さを消して', 'humanize', '文章をリライトして' ([ahandsel/tokyo-hiker](https://github.com/ahandsel/tokyo-hiker/tree/HEAD/skills/ja-review-text) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **ja-revise**: 日本語の文書や設定ファイル（CLAUDE.md、ルール、スキル、エージェント、コマンド、README、仕様書、設計書など）を点検し、読み手が迷う文と箇条書きを直す。曖昧な文、不完全な文、冗長な文、抽象的な文を直す。特に、動作主が分からない文、動作対象が分からない文、動詞の修飾語が足りない文、修飾語が修飾先から離れた文、前提を書かずに本題に入っている説明を直す。箇条書きが並列か、番号が順序を表しているか、並列する項目の粒度が揃っているかも確かめる。ユーザーに「日本語を直して」「文章を校正して」「推敲して」「読みやすくして」「曖昧な文を直して」と言われたときに使う。新しく書いたスキルやエージェントの文を見直すときにも使う。点検は ja-reviewer エージェントに分担させ、修正案をユーザーに示して承認を得てから直す。 ([hanzitong/dotfiles](https://github.com/hanzitong/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dotfiles/claude/skills/ja-revise))
- **ja-skill-design-workflow**: 日本語で高精度なSkillを設計・改善するための手順化スキル。要件ヒアリング、利用シナリオ定義、SKILL.md設計、references分割、トリガー検証、品質レビュー、反復改善までを案内する。要件の抜け漏れ確認や曖昧要求の切り分けも含め、設計根拠を残して仕上げる。skill作成を依頼されたとき、既存skillの品質を上げたいとき、ポン出しを避けて設計根拠付きで仕上げたいときに使用する。単なるコード実装依頼や翻訳依頼のみが目的のときは使用しない。 ([Sigumaa/skills](https://github.com/Sigumaa/skills/tree/HEAD/ja-skill-design-workflow) · ★1)
- **ja-slides**: 日本語のスライドを、どれも同じに見える「生成 AI っぽい」見た目にせずに作る。用途と見せ方を最初に聞き、見本 3 案から選んでもらい、色・書体・字の大きさを deck.lock.json に固定して、型（レイアウト）を組み合わせて 1 枚の HTML にする。点検スクリプトではみ出し・小さすぎる字・強調の多さ・同じ型の連続を見つけ、PDF と一覧画像も出す。「スライドを作って」「発表資料」「勉強会の資料」「この記事をスライドに」「プレゼン」と言われたとき、PowerPoint ファイルが必須でないときに使う。 ([OTA-EITA/skills](https://github.com/OTA-EITA/skills/tree/HEAD/ja-slides))
- **ja-slop-review**: Review and rewrite Japanese (and JP-EN mixed) prose to remove "AI slop" — over-smooth uniform tone, generic buzzwords, weak agency, ritual hedging, false contrasts, propositional headings, and other generated-text tells. Use when writing, editing, reviewing, or scoring Japanese articles, memos, briefings, docs, release notes, social posts, or public-facing copy, or when asked to make text "less AI-ish / 人間が書いたように". ([ystk-kai/yokai-harness](https://github.com/ystk-kai/yokai-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/ja-slop-review) · 2 copies)
- **ja-so-seo-do-u-mi**: Use when the user wants to revise, diagnose, rewrite, shorten, lengthen, or polish a Korean job application essay or 자기소개서 using a pasted 채용공고/JD, 자기소개서 문항, 글자수 제한, and rough draft. Default to preserving the user's intended appeal points unless they explicitly ask for a full rewrite. Trigger on requests such as "자소서 수정", "자기소개서 고쳐줘", "채용공고에 맞게 자소서", "JD 기반 자소서", "문장만 다듬어줘", "글자수 맞춰줘", "AI 티 안 나게 자소서", "기업 제출용으로 다듬어줘", and "ja-so-seo-do-u-mi". ([gwagjiug/ja-so-seo-do-u-mi](https://github.com/gwagjiug/ja-so-seo-do-u-mi/tree/HEAD/skills/ja-so-seo-do-u-mi) · ★47)
- **ja-srt**: Transcribe Japanese audio/video to zh-TW (Taiwan) subtitles with whisper.cpp, or fix subtitles that linger on screen during silence. ([Andrew781123/dotfiles](https://github.com/Andrew781123/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/opencode/skills/ja-srt))
- **ja-subject-predicate-check**: 日本語の説明文から主語と述語の骨格を抜き出し、主述のねじれを検出して修正する。対象はファイル、引数の文章、会話中の発言、直前の出力のいずれでもよい。MR説明・設計文書・コメントに使用する。 ([kumisun/ja-subject-predicate-check](https://github.com/kumisun/ja-subject-predicate-check/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **ja-tech-edit-score-check**: 開いている Markdown 原稿について、推敲ブランチの編集内容が ja-tech-edit-score 選好評価モデルに 沿っているか hunk 単位で採点する。ユーザーが「推敲チェック」「選好チェック」 「pref チェック」と言ったとき、または edit/* ブランチ上の章ファイルの選好スコアを 確認したいときに使う。 ([k16shikano/ja-tech-edit-score](https://github.com/k16shikano/ja-tech-edit-score/tree/HEAD/skills/ja-tech-edit-score-check) · ★14)
- **ja-technical-style-guide**: Use this skill when writing, translating, reviewing, or editing Japanese programming documentation. It normalizes headings, step-by-step instructions, figure captions, terminology, particles, API identifiers, and technical formatting to consistent Japanese. ([aspose-pdf/Aspose.PDF-Documentation](https://github.com/aspose-pdf/Aspose.PDF-Documentation/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/ja-technical-style-guide) · ★2)
- **ja-technical-writing**: Coppiceリポジトリ内で日本語の技術説明・手順・設計記録を作成または推敲するときに使う。技術上の意味を保ち、ジャンルに合う自然な日本語へ整える。 ([fertile-org/coppice](https://github.com/fertile-org/coppice/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ja-technical-writing) · ★81)
- **ja-technical-writing**: 公開する日本語の技術文書(チュートリアル・ハウツー・解説)を書く・直す・レビューするときに必ず参照する。表記(全角半角・数字・記号・カタカナ長音)・論理(用語導入・圧縮の禁止・根拠)・Geolonia社内の言葉遣いの3層で規則化してある。「技術文書」「ドキュメント品質」「textlint」「体言止め」と言われたら参照する。 ([yuiseki/ai-secretary](https://github.com/yuiseki/ai-secretary/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ja-technical-writing) · ★1)
- **ja-text-communication**: ユーザーとのあらゆるコミュニケーションの最初に、何かを書く・出力する前に必ず参照すること。チャット応答・レポート・解説・作業報告・ドキュメントなど、文章を書く・出力するすべての場面に適用される。 ([mathbullet/skills](https://github.com/mathbullet/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/ja-text-communication/skills/ja-text-communication) · ★199 · 3 copies)
- **ja-translate**: Translate Japanese text to English and Vietnamese. Invoke when user shares Japanese text, Japanese file, or asks to translate a GitHub PR/issue written in Japanese. Supports: plain text, file path, PR number (--pr), issue number (--issue). Applies personal writing style from ~/.claude/personal/style.md if available. Output: EN first (accurate/literal), then VI (natural, styled). ([githoaitandev/agents-kit](https://github.com/githoaitandev/agents-kit/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ja-translate) · 2 copies)
- **ja-v3-completion-orchestrator**: Orchestrate J&A Client Essential production-completion work across subagents. Use for dependency planning, work-packet delegation, ownership, review loops, integration gates, or any task spanning multiple J&A domains. Do not use for a trivial single-file fix. ([Kripta-Studios/ja-automation-platform](https://github.com/Kripta-Studios/ja-automation-platform/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ja-v3-completion-orchestrator))
- **ja-writing**: 人が読む日本語の文 (アプリの UI の文言・App Store の説明文・ヘルプ・issue・コミットメッセージ・コードのコメント・ユーザーへの報告) を、主語や目的語が抜けず、機能を利用者の動作で言い表す自然な日本語で書き、書いた後に ja-textlint (dotfiles の bin/) で機械の検査を通す。AI が書きがちな比喩の語 (〜側に倒す・黙って・効く・版) を起きていることの言葉に直し、否定対比 (A ではなく B)・道具の擬人化・前置きと定型の結びを整え、手直しでは意味を変えず情報を足さない。「自然な日本語にして」「AI っぽい日本語を直して」「日本語がおかしい」「主語がない」「日本語を直して」「文言を見直して」「ja-writing」で発火。英語の文の翻訳だけでなく、日本語で一から書くときにも使う。誤字・表記揺れだけの校正なら ja-textlint を単独で回せば足りる。 ([jiikko/dotfiles](https://github.com/jiikko/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/_claude/skills/ja-writing) · ★2)
- **ja-writing**: tanteki・natural-japanese・yomiyasu の3スキルを決まった順序で通し、日本語文書を作成・推敲する。利用者が ja-writing の名前を出して頼んだときや、3スキルをまとめて使うよう求められたときに使う。単体スキルの指定には使わない ([fussy113/fussy113-agent-plugins](https://github.com/fussy113/fussy113-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/ja-writing/skills/ja-writing) · ★1)
- **ja-writing**: 日本語の文章を、textlintによる機械的な検査と、文章を書いた経緯を知らない別のエージェントによるレビューの2段階で確認する。 次のときに使う。 (1) README・ドキュメント・コードコメント等の日本語の文章を書いたとき。 (2) コミットメッセージ・Issue・Pull Requestの本文を日本語で書いたとき。 (3) 日本語で書かれた既存の文章のレビューを依頼されたとき。 (4) textlintで日本語の文書を検査・修正するとき。 textlintの実行と結果への対応、AIレビューの依頼方法、誤検出の抑制方法を扱う。 ([cffnpwr/agent-toolkit](https://github.com/cffnpwr/agent-toolkit/tree/HEAD/.apm/skills/ja-writing) · 3 copies)
- **ja-writing**: 人が読む日本語の書き方の規則と、Gemini 4 Neon での仕上げの手順。提案資料・議事録・メール・Slack の文面・Issue/PR の本文・README・設計書・手順書・説明のページを書くとき、図やファイルのパスを入れるとき、項目を削れと言われたとき、ja-review などの hook に指摘されたときに使う。コード・コミットメッセージ・エージェント向けの指示文には使わない。 ([ShoFukada/agent-harness](https://github.com/ShoFukada/agent-harness/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/ja-writing))
- **ja-writing**: 日本語文章の参照資料を 2 層 (このスキル内の蒸留版 references/*.md と、~/.local/share/yokai-harness/references/ の原典 clone) で使うためのスキル。日本語文書の表記規約 (用字用語・全角半角・日英混在のスペース・記号) を決めるとき、textlint / prh の規則やプリセットを選定・設定するとき、文書種別 (議事録・調査レポート・社内ガイド・UI 文言・エラーメッセージ・ヘルプ・リリースノート) ごとの必須要素と構成を確認するとき、構文の読解負荷 (二重否定・係り受け・名詞化) ややさしい日本語の規則を引くとき、日本語 AI 臭の個別規則の出所・閾値・線引きを原典で確かめるときに、まず蒸留版を読み、索引が指す原典ファイルだけを深掘りする。文章のレビュー・採点・書き直しそのものは ja-slop-review の担当で、このスキルは手順を持たない。 ([ystk-kai/yokai-harness](https://github.com/ystk-kai/yokai-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/ja-writing) · 2 copies)
- **ja-writing-humanizer**: 日本語を主対象に、英語や日英混在の下書きから LLM 臭さ、反復、冗長さ、翻訳調、宣伝調、表記ゆれを取り除き、意味・事実・文体を保ったまま読み手に自然で美しい文章へ整える。Use when the user asks to humanize, de-AI, polish, rewrite, or make prose sound less machine-generated. Applies to general writing such as articles, essays, announcements, product copy, email, reports, notes, social posts, and mixed Japanese/English text; not limited to technical writing. ([viasnake/ja-writing-humanizer](https://github.com/viasnake/ja-writing-humanizer/tree/HEAD/skills/ja-writing-humanizer))
- **ja-writing-rules**: Use when writing or revising Japanese prose a human will read — reports back to a user, README and design documents, specifications, release notes, and notices for non-engineers. - **ja-zhtw-translation**: 日本語と台湾華語（台湾で使われる繁体字の中国語）を双方向に翻訳するためのスキル。Twitter/Discord/オンラインゲームなどのチャットやSNSの会話を中心に、日本語から中国語、中国語から日本語への翻訳、訳文のニュアンス確認を頼まれたときに使う。 ([hoshimiyacelica/skills](https://github.com/hoshimiyacelica/skills/tree/HEAD/ja-zhtw-translation))
- **ja3-fingerprint**: JA3 identifies threats using TLS fingerprints. Signals: asset mapping, subdomain. ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/tool-skills/ja3-fingerprint))
- **jaah**: Use when the user asks how to do something in Jaah, or about Jaah tasks, epics, chats, schedules, automations, triggers, credentials or secrets, nodes, projects, worktrees or slots, notifications, personas, playbooks or templates — and whenever the agent could do it itself through the jaah control tools (mcp__plugin_jaah_control__*). - **jaar-argument-development**: Use when building the central argument of a Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) article. JAAR explicitly wants an essay that "has a point" — analysis with a contestable thesis, not description or survey. Structures the argument; it does not gather the sources. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-argument-development) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-citation-and-style**: Use when formatting citations and references for a Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) article. JAAR's distinctive rule is IN-TEXT author-date citations (NOT footnote citations) with an alphabetized year-keyed reference list; footnotes are reserved for substantive points. Handles reference apparatus; it does not write the argument. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-citation-and-style) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-review-process**: Use to understand how the Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) evaluates a manuscript — editor pre-screening for broad significance, double-blind review, a roughly 90% rejection rate, a months-long timeline, and a commissioned-only book-review section. Sets expectations and shapes the article to survive; it does not contact editors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-review-process) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-revision-and-response**: Use when revising a Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) article after a revise-and-resubmit and writing the response to readers' reports. Reviewers come from different traditions and methods, so the response must satisfy each on its own terms while protecting the article's argument and its broad significance. Plans the revision and response; it does not fabricate sources. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-revision-and-response) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-scholarly-positioning**: Use when positioning a Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) article in the scholarly conversation so it reads as a contribution to the study of religion broadly, not a footnote to one subfield. Stakes the intervention across traditions and methods; it does not write the literature review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-scholarly-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-sources-and-evidence**: Use when handling the evidence base of a Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) article — primary texts, traditions, historical documents, material/visual sources, or ethnographic fieldwork. Plans rigorous, transparent source work for humanities scholarship in the study of religion; it does not run statistics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-sources-and-evidence) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-structure-and-exposition**: Use when organizing a Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) article so a method-conscious argument unfolds clearly within the ~8,000-12,000-word range (including references and footnotes). Builds the essay's architecture; it does not generate content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-structure-and-exposition) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for the Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) via ScholarOne / Manuscript Central — anonymized main document plus separate title page, the broad-significance framing, length and abstract limits, in-text citation style, and house conventions. Final checks; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-submission) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-theory-and-method**: Use when articulating the theory and method of a Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) article. JAAR prizes method-conscious, reflexive, non-confessional scholarship and explicitly publishes studies of the methodologies by which religion is explored. Sharpens the theoretical/methodological frame; it does not gather sources. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-theory-and-method) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a project fits the Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) and how to frame it. JAAR's bar is broad and fundamental interest to the study of religion across traditions and methods — a contribution only to one subfield will be returned for reframing before review. Frames the question; it does not do the research. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-workflow**: Use as the entry point for any Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) manuscript. Routes to the right JAAR sub-skill by lifecycle stage, and enforces the journal's gatekeeping demand up front — the article must speak to a question of broad and fundamental interest to the study of religion, not just a subfield. It dispatches; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-American-Academy-of-Religion-Skills/skills/jaar-workflow) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **jaar-writing-style**: Use when drafting or polishing the prose of a Journal of the American Academy of Religion (JAAR) article so it is clear, analytic, gender-neutral, and readable across the study of religion. - **jaccard-index-threshold-filtering**: Use when preparing RNA-seq simulation inputs and you must restrict candidate genes to a biologically coherent, GO-annotated set (e.g. protein-coding genes on a single chromosome such as chr22) before count-matrix generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/jaccard-index-threshold-filtering))
- **jaccard-kernel-computation-single-cell**: Use when scATAC-seq data must be clustered without predefined peaks: cell-by-bin count matrices (e.g. 5-kb bins in a .snap file) need conversion to a pair-wise cell similarity kernel for dimensionality reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-kernel-computation-single-cell))
- **jaccard-mask-overlap-evaluation**: Use when an IFM image has been warped into the MALDI MSI frame (e.g. via ANTs SyNRA in msiFlow's msi_if_registration_flow) and you need a quantitative overlap metric to validate registration accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-mask-overlap-evaluation))
- **jaccard-maximum-common-subgraph-similarity**: Use when lipid species names must be compared by molecular structure rather than exact name matching, e.g. to build structural distance matrices across heterogeneous lipidomes from csv/xlsx or mzTab-M lipidomics tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-maximum-common-subgraph-similarity))
- **jaccard-ordination-health-status**: Use when MASST matches of synthesized compounds have been joined to ReDU sample metadata (biospecimen/tissue, health phenotype) and you need to test whether sample groups cluster by health status or tissue based on shared detected conjugates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-ordination-health-status))
- **jaccard-overlap-computation**: Use when you have per-run OTU abundance tables or OTU call lists (e.g., MAPseq 'map.otutable' TSVs from multiple .mseq files) and need to quantify between-run reproducibility of OTU identifications via Jaccard overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/jaccard-overlap-computation))
- **jaccard-overlap-of-shared-otu-identifications**: Use when the same amplicon samples have been sequenced or processed in two independent runs and you need to quantify how consistently a tool (MAPseq, VSEARCH, NINJA-OPS, USEARCH) assigns the same OTUs per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/jaccard-overlap-of-shared-otu-identifications))
- **jaccard-overlap-scoring-of-peak-sets**: Use when a set of significant 1D NMR peaks (ppm values) has been clustered by Pearson correlation and you must rank HMDB metabolite matches by how well their expected peak patterns overlap the cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-overlap-scoring-of-peak-sets))
- **jaccard-peak-overlap-scoring**: Use when ranking candidate compounds for an unknown GC/EI-MS analyte where cosine spectrum similarity alone leaves the true compound below rank 1, and DeepEI-predicted fingerprints (msp-merged) are available for the analyte and enumerated candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-peak-overlap-scoring))
- **jaccard-reaction-set-overlap-analysis**: Use when comparing the reaction content of corresponding KEGG reference pathways in two organisms, and you need a normalized per-pathway similarity in [0,1] computed over reaction sets or multisets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-reaction-set-overlap-analysis))
- **jaccard-score**: Compute the jaccard_score metric — provided by scikit-learn. Use when the user has predictions and ground-truth and needs to compute jaccard_score, or asks how to score with jaccard_score. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/jaccard-score) · ★3)
- **jaccard-score-cross-validation**: Use when evaluating a sparse-positive molecular site-of-metabolism classifier (e.g. ~5% reactive bonds) where accuracy is mis and per-class/isoform Jaccard scores from external cross-validation are the appropriate metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-score-cross-validation))
- **jaccard-score-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating a bond- or site-level predictor whose output is a set of predicted locations (e.g., BoMX−Y pairs or SoMs) per molecule, and you need a single per-class score that penalizes false positives and false negatives symmetrically. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-score-metric-computation))
- **jaccard-set-similarity-on-reaction-sets**: Use when two organisms' metabolisms have been reconstructed from KEGG KGML into per-pathway reaction sets and you need quantitative local and global functional similarity between them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-set-similarity-on-reaction-sets))
- **jaccard-similarity-analysis-of-regulon-rankings**: Use when two gene regulatory network inference tools (e.g., MINI-EX and SCENIC) have produced per-cell-type regulons and you need to quantify their overlap per cluster and validate specific regulon TF calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-similarity-analysis-of-regulon-rankings))
- **jaccard-similarity-assessment-of-de-reproducibility**: Use when you must quantify robustness/reproducibility of differential expression or isoform usage calls across unbalanced single-cell groups, by resampling equal cell numbers per group and comparing significant sets across replicates with Jaccard indices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/jaccard-similarity-assessment-of-de-reproducibility))
- **jaccard-similarity-cluster-comparison**: Use when two clustering solutions (e.g., SRIQ vs consensus clustering) exist on the same sample set and you need a quantitative, per-K similarity metric for the derived cluster partitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-similarity-cluster-comparison))
- **jaccard-similarity-computation**: Use when you have ranked TR output tables from multiple TR identification methods applied to the same epigenomic query region sets and need to quantify overlap of their top-ranked predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-similarity-computation))
- **jaccard-similarity-computation**: Use when scoring isomer-pair MS/MS similarity from multi-collision-energy spectra (e.g., NCE 1–100%) to build I/N-ratio-based identification criteria, or when comparing Jaccard vs dot product/entropy sensitivity-specificity on spectral libraries like CQMD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-similarity-computation--6428ed20d4337e9b))
- **jaccard-similarity-computation**: Use when quantifying metabolite-content overlap between pathway pairs — e.g., building a pairwise Jaccard redundancy matrix across KEGG, Reactome, and WikiPathways pathways stored in RaMP to identify which database pairs show the largest overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-similarity-computation--eb72e54c71d51c2a))
- **jaccard-similarity-computation**: Use when comparing which interactions are shared versus distinct across CCC inference methods, resources, or datasets — e.g., to measure how much top-ranked predictions from CellChat vs NATMI (or CellChatDB vs Ramilowski) overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-similarity-computation--6934db20a80cb0df))
- **jaccard-similarity-computation**: Use when quantifying overlap between marker gene sets for the same cell types across multiple marker databases or cell-sorting marker collections to assess database consistency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-similarity-computation--ec8e2ebfbd2d9147))
- **jaccard-similarity-computation-on-read-sets**: Use when you need to quantify concordance between two or more read-level metagenomic classifiers by comparing their classified-read sets with Jaccard similarity and per-tool missed-read tallies. - **jaccard-similarity-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when clustering sparse single-cell ATAC-seq profiles without population-level peak calls: raw cell-by-bin (5kb/10kb) accessibility matrices or .snap AM matrices need a cell-level embedding before Leiden clustering. - **jaccard-similarity-index-computation**: Use when the goal is to quantify how similar the metabolisms of two (or more) KEGG organisms are, either locally (per reference pathway) or globally (whole metabolism), e. - **jaccard-similarity-matrix-computation**: Use when you have binary gene membership sets (e.g., per-tissue DE gene lists from the MoTrPAC rat EET study) and need to measure how concordant or tissue-specific the sets are across conditions or tissues. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-similarity-matrix-computation))
- **jaccard-similarity-matrix-construction**: Use when comparing label vocabularies across published single-cell references (e.g. is HCC 'Lymphoid-B' closer to naive or memory B cells?) or harmonizing subtype annotations from multiple atlases. - **jaccard-similarity-matrix-construction**: Use when a pathway enrichment analysis needs inter-pathway dependencies quantified from gene set overlaps — e.g. before fitting pareg with network regularization, or for rendering a pathway network view — and the input is a term-to-gene mapping (df_terms) such as GO BP terms of size 50-500. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-similarity-matrix-construction--91acdfb8c7fa14e4))
- **jaccard-similarity-module-overlap-quantification**: Use when two PyWGCNA objects (e.g., the 5xFAD and 3xTg-AD networks) have been built with module assignments and you need to find which module pairs significantly overlap, indicating conserved co-expression signatures across datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-similarity-module-overlap-quantification))
- **jaccard-similarity-of-top-ranked-regulators**: Use when comparing activity-inference methods (e.g. the 11 decoupleR methods) on the same omics benchmark to measure whether methods nominate the same top regulators, complementing median Spearman correlation of activity scores. - **jaccard-similarity-scoring-multi-collision-energy-fragments**: Use when query MS/MS spectra are acquired at multiple collision energies and must be matched to multi-CE reference spectra (e.g. Exploris 480 queries vs. CQMD Exploris 120 references) to call metabolite identifications under Figure 2 cutoff criteria. - **jade-recipe-1.7-1.8-build-rmic-deprecated**: Documents (does not remove) the two &lt;rmic&gt; Ant task uses that are deprecated for removal starting with JDK 8 and removed outright in JDK 9+. Agent-mode recipe: the correct action on a 1.7-&gt;1.8 jump is a deferral marker plus a real build check, not deletion of a still-working task. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/1.7-to-1.8/jade-recipe-1.7-1.8-build-rmic-deprecated) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-1.7-1.8-jdbc-odbc-bridge**: Reviews a default sun.jdbc.odbc.JdbcOdbcDriver reference removed from the JDK in Java 8. Agent-mode recipe: there is no mechanical replacement, so this document defines the judgment call (fail fast vs. defer to config) and the invariant that only the literal default value may change. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/1.7-to-1.8/jade-recipe-1.7-1.8-jdbc-odbc-bridge) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-1.7-1.8-lambda-conversion**: Converts anonymous single-abstract-method (SAM) inner classes to Java 8 lambda expressions. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the files and flagged sites; this document defines the transform, the non-convertible categories that must be SKIPPED, and the invariants. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/1.7-to-1.8/jade-recipe-1.7-1.8-lambda-conversion) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-1.7-1.8-thread-stop-removal**: Inspects .stop() calls flagged by THREAD_STOP_DISABLED for actual Thread.stop() usage. Most calls are custom .stop() methods in JADE's own classes (Timer.stop(), client.stop(), HTTPServer.stop()), not java.lang.Thread.stop(Throwable). Marks false positives as SKIPPED. If actual Thread.stop() is found, defers for manual review via JADE-MODERNIZATION-DEFERRED marker. Invoked by jade-core-rule-dispatcher. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/1.7-to-1.8/jade-recipe-1.7-1.8-thread-stop-removal) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-1.7-diamond-operator**: Replaces explicit generic type arguments with Java 7 diamond operator (&lt;&gt;). Scans +/-2 lines for the pattern, skips already-converted lines. Removes JADE-FLAG:DIAMOND_OPERATOR comments. Atomic write. Invoked by jade-core-rule-dispatcher. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/1.7/jade-recipe-1.7-diamond-operator) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-1.7-strings-in-switch**: Converts .equals() if-chains to Java 7 switch statements. Handles sequential if-return and if-else-if-else patterns. Defers unconvertible single branches via JADE-MODERNIZATION-DEFERRED. Removes JADE-FLAG:STRINGS_IN_SWITCH comments on success. Invoked by jade-core-rule-dispatcher. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/1.7/jade-recipe-1.7-strings-in-switch) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-1.7-try-with-resources**: Converts manual open/close resource management to Java 7 try-with-resources where — and only where — the resource genuinely implements Closeable/AutoCloseable, is closed unconditionally on every path, and is never touched again after the try. Agent-mode recipe: scripts/scan.py narrows the manifest's broad TRY_WITH_RESOURCES flags (0.6% precision) to a shortlist (~70% precision); this document defines the judgment calls the shortlist cannot make on its own. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/1.7/jade-recipe-1.7-try-with-resources) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-11-17-applet-api-removal**: Handles JADE's dependency on java.applet.Applet, which JEP 398 deprecated for removal in JDK 17. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the files; this document defines what may be changed mechanically and why the removal decision itself belongs to the user. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/11-to-17/jade-recipe-11-17-applet-api-removal) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-11-17-multicast-group-api**: Moves JADE's multicast main-container discovery off the deprecated MulticastSocket.joinGroup(InetAddress)/leaveGroup(InetAddress) overloads that JDK 17 superseded. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the file; this document defines the transform and the one argument the agent must not invent. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/11-to-17/jade-recipe-11-17-multicast-group-api) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-11-17-rmic-task-removal**: Removes the two Ant &lt;rmic&gt; pregeneration steps from build.xml. The rmic tool they drive was removed in JDK 15, and Ant refuses the task on JDK 15+, which is the only thing stopping the JADE build on JDK 17. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the file; this document defines the transform and the one condition under which the agent must stop instead. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/11-to-17/jade-recipe-11-17-rmic-task-removal) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-11-17-threadgroup-destroy**: Removes the single ThreadGroup.destroy() call in jade.core.Runtime's shutdown path. JDK 16 terminally deprecated it and calls the destruction mechanism inherently flawed. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the file; this document defines the transform and the diagnostic it must not silently drop. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/11-to-17/jade-recipe-11-17-threadgroup-destroy) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-11-17-wrapper-valueof**: Replaces primitive wrapper constructor calls with the corresponding valueOf factory. JEP 390 (JDK 16) deprecated those constructors for removal. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the files; this document defines the transform and the identity-sensitive sites it must refuse to touch. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/11-to-17/jade-recipe-11-17-wrapper-valueof) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-17-21-file-io-charset**: Pins an explicit UTF-8 charset on the file reader, writer and print stream constructors that JADE uses to write files it later reads back itself. JEP 400 (JDK 18) changed those constructors from the platform default charset to UTF-8. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the files; this document defines the transform and the sites it must refuse to touch. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/17-to-21/jade-recipe-17-21-file-io-charset) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-17-21-finalize-removal**: Removes Object.finalize() overrides in favour of the close or shutdown method each of them already delegates to. JEP 421 (JDK 18) deprecated finalization for removal. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the files; this document defines the transform and the condition that forces NEEDS_REVIEW. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/17-to-21/jade-recipe-17-21-finalize-removal) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-17-21-threaddeath-removal**: Removes the catch of java.lang.ThreadDeath, which JDK 20 deprecated for removal after making Thread.stop() throw UnsupportedOperationException. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the file; this document defines the transform and what must be preserved. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/17-to-21/jade-recipe-17-21-threaddeath-removal) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-8-11-cldr-locale-data**: Makes date/time formatting independent of the default locale data provider, which switched from JRE/COMPAT to CLDR in JDK 9. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the files; this document defines the two accepted fixes and which one the agent may apply. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/8-to-11/jade-recipe-8-11-cldr-locale-data) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-8-11-corba-removal**: Deletes JADE's CORBA dependency instead of retrofitting it: the generated FIPA IDL stubs, the IIOP message transport protocol, and their build.xml wiring. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the files; this document defines what is deleted, what is left alone, and how the leftover references are resolved. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/8-to-11/jade-recipe-8-11-corba-removal) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-8-11-corba-retrofit**: Java 11 removed the java.corba module (JEP 320): org.omg.* classes no longer exist in the JDK. This recipe retrofits a standalone GlassFish CORBA implementation (omgapi + orb + transitive jars) into lib/corba/ and wires it into the Ant compile classpath so FIPA/* and jade.mtp.iiop/* compile and run on JDK 11. Idempotent. Removes JADE-FLAG:CORBA_REMOVAL markers. Invoked by jade-core-rule-dispatcher. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/8-to-11/jade-recipe-8-11-corba-retrofit) · ★2)
- **jade-recipe-8-11-tls-anon-cipher**: Resolves hard-coded anonymous TLS cipher suites (DH_anon / ECDH_anon), which JDK 11 disables by default through jdk.tls.disabledAlgorithms. Agent-mode recipe: the shard contract names the files; this document defines the transform, its limit, and when the fix stops being the agent's call. ([TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite](https://github.com/TheRealSeber/PolishedJADEite/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/java-migration-skill-registry/8-to-11/jade-recipe-8-11-tls-anon-cipher) · ★2)
